Company brief

COPYCAT AS is a private limited company operating in Trykking ellers. Latest accounts (2025): revenue NOK 61.6m, annual result NOK 2.9m. Annual accounts available for 2011-2025 (15 years). Detailed records only available as PDFs. Revenue shrinking (-5.1% YoY). Registered employees: 34. Baseline run, no prior version to compare against (run none). Unknowns: hiring (no job sources were checked).
